================= FILE: src/app/page.tsx =================
// Hermes Web — interface de chat type ChatGPT pour l'agent Hermes de Nous Research.
// Layout : la conversation reste le chef d'orchestre ; l'artefact est un objet
// de première classe — sidebar fluide à gauche, chat au centre, panneau artefact
// à droite (≈ 45 %) qui s'ouvre automatiquement à la naissance d'un artefact.
// Mobile : cartes inline + visionneuse plein écran avec swipe.
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import type { ImperativePanelHandle } from "react-resizable-panels";
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetTitle } from "@/components/ui/sheet";
import {
  ResizableHandle,
  ResizablePanel,
  ResizablePanelGroup,
} from "@/components/ui/resizable";
import { useToast } from "@/hooks/use-toast";
import { useProviders } from "@/hooks/use-providers";
import { useSystemPrompts } from "@/hooks/use-system-prompts";
import { useMemory } from "@/hooks/use-memory";
import { useIsMobile } from "@/hooks/use-mobile";
import { getSocket } from "@/lib/socket";
import { Sidebar } from "@/components/chat/sidebar";
import { SettingsPanel } from "@/components/settings/settings-panel";
import { SystemPromptPanel } from "@/components/settings/system-prompt-panel";
import { ChatHeader, type ExportFormat } from "@/components/chat/chat-header";
import { MessageList } from "@/components/chat/message-list";
import { Composer } from "@/components/chat/composer";
import { CommandPalette } from "@/components/chat/command-palette";
import { DebugBridge } from "@/components/chat/debug-bridge";
import { ArtifactPanel, ArtifactViewerDialog } from "@/components/chat/artifact-panel";
import { useHermesChat } from "@/hooks/use-hermes-chat";
import { streamStore } from "@/lib/stream-store";
import { exportJson, exportMarkdown, exportPdf } from "@/lib/export";
import { flashTitle, playCompletionBeep, setGeneratingTitle } from "@/lib/notify";
import { onSettingsChanged, readClientSetting, writeClientSetting } from "@/lib/settings";
import type { Artifact, ShareView, TurnTools } from "@/lib/chat-types";

const SIDEBAR_WIDTH_KEY = "fork.ui.sidebar.width"; // registre des réglages (FORK.md §6)

export default function HermesWebPage() {
  // La page orchestre deux hooks : le chat (source de vérité) et les prompts
  // système (F-11/F-19). Le prompt actif de la conversation est injecté dans
  // la jauge de contexte du composer (poste « Prompt de conversation »).
  const chat = useHermesChat();
  const promptsApi = useSystemPrompts(chat.activeId);
  // Mémoire utilisateur (F-18) — section Hermes du volet Paramètres.
  const memoryApi = useMemory();
  const { toast } = useToast();
  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false);
  const [paletteOpen, setPaletteOpen] = useState(false);
  /** Visionneuse plein écran (mobile uniquement). */
  const [viewerOpen, setViewerOpen] = useState(false);
  /** Sheet Paramètres (engrenage de la carte de statut — FORK.md §7.4). */
  const [settingsOpen, setSettingsOpen] = useState(false);
  /** Sheet « Prompt système de la conversation » (F-11). */
  const [promptOpen, setPromptOpen] = useState(false);
  const providersApi = useProviders();
  /** F-09 — une clé Tavily/Exa active rend le toggle « Web » du composer actionnable. */
  const webSearchAvailable = useMemo(
    () =>
      providersApi.providers.some(
        (p) =>
          p.kind === "service" &&
          /tavily|exa/i.test(p.name) &&
          p.keys.some((k) => k.status === "ok" || k.status === "untested"),
      ),
    [providersApi.providers],
  );
  const sidebarRef = useRef<ImperativePanelHandle>(null);

  // ── Partage public lecture seule (F-13) ────────────────────────────────
  // Un seul lien actif par conversation ; le bus share:changed (broadcast)
  // maintient l'état à jour, le changement de conversation recharge (l'ack
  // repart de zéro : null tant que la réponse n'est pas arrivée).
  const [share, setShare] = useState<ShareView | null>(null);
  const activeIdRef = useRef<string | null>(chat.activeId);
  useEffect(() => {
    activeIdRef.current = chat.activeId;
  }, [chat.activeId]);
  // F-03 : purge du lien de partage au changement de conversation, pendant
  // le rendu (motif React « adjust state on prop change » — pas d'effet,
  // l'ancien `if (!id) return` laissait le lien de la conversation
  // précédente affiché quand activeId passait à null).
  const [shareConvId, setShareConvId] = useState<string | null>(chat.activeId);
  if (chat.activeId !== shareConvId) {
    setShareConvId(chat.activeId);
    setShare(null);
  }
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = chat.activeId;
    if (!id) return;
    const socket = getSocket();
    socket.emit(
      "share:get",
      { conversationId: id },
      (res: { share?: ShareView | null } = {}) => {
        // F-03 : garde par ref (l'ancienne comparaison comparait la
        // variable de la MÊME closure — toujours vraie si l'ack arrivait
        // après un changement de conversation).
        if (id !== activeIdRef.current) return;
        setShare(res.share ?? null);
      }
    );
  }, [chat.activeId]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const socket = getSocket();
    const onShareChanged = (p: { conversationId: string; share?: ShareView | null }) => {
      if (p.conversationId !== chat.activeId) return;
      setShare(p.share ?? null);
    };
    socket.on("share:changed", onShareChanged);
    return () => {
      socket.off("share:changed", onShareChanged);
    };
  }, [chat.activeId]);

  /** Crée le lien public (snapshot figé) et copie l'URL dans le presse-papier. */
  const handleShareCreate = useCallback(async (): Promise<ShareView | null> => {
    const id = chat.activeId;
    if (!id) return null;
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      getSocket().emit(
        "share:create",
        { conversationId: id },
        (res: { share?: ShareView; error?: string } = {}) => {
          if (res.share) {
            setShare(res.share);
            const url = `${window.location.origin}/shared/${res.share.token}`;
            navigator.clipboard
              ?.writeText(url)
              .then(() => {
                toast({
                  title: "Lien de partage créé et copié",
                  description: `${res.share!.messageCount} message(s) figés — lecture seule, révocable à tout moment.`,
                  duration: 4000,
                });
              })
              .catch(() => {
                toast({
                  title: "Lien de partage créé",
                  description: url,
                  duration: 6000,
                });
              });
          } else if (res.error) {
            toast({
              title: "Partage impossible",
              description: res.error,
              variant: "destructive",
            });
          }
          resolve(res.share ?? null);
        }
      );
    });
  }, [chat.activeId, toast]);

  /** Révoque le lien actif (le destinataire voit « lien révoqué »). */
  const handleShareRevoke = useCallback(() => {
    const id = chat.activeId;
    if (!id) return;
    getSocket().emit(
      "share:revoke",
      { conversationId: id },
      (res: { ok?: boolean } = {}) => {
        if (res.ok) {
          setShare(null);
          toast({ title: "Lien révoqué", description: "La vue publique n'est plus accessible.", duration: 3000 });
        }
      }
    );
  }, [chat.activeId, toast]);

  const activeConversation = useMemo(
    () => chat.conversations.find((c) => c.id === chat.activeId) ?? null,
    [chat.conversations, chat.activeId]
  );

  // ── Sidebar fluide : restaure la largeur mémorisée (registre fork) ───
  // Garde-fou : la librairie émet `onResize` dès le montage (taille par défaut) —
  // sans ce drapeau, elle écraserait la largeur persistée avant sa restauration.
  const sidebarRestoredRef = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const pct = readClientSetting<number>(SIDEBAR_WIDTH_KEY);
    if (Number.isFinite(pct) && pct >= 12 && pct <= 34) {
      sidebarRef.current?.resize(pct);
    }
    sidebarRestoredRef.current = true;
  }, []);

  const handleSidebarResize = useCallback((size: number) => {
    if (!Number.isFinite(size)) return;
    if (!sidebarRestoredRef.current) return; // taille initiale au montage
    writeClientSetting(SIDEBAR_WIDTH_KEY, size);
  }, []);

  // ── Largeur de sidebar : application immédiate depuis le sheet ────────
  // (le garde-fou évite la boucle : le panneau réémet sa taille à l'écriture)
  useEffect(() => {
    return onSettingsChanged(({ key, value }) => {
      if (key !== SIDEBAR_WIDTH_KEY || typeof value !== "number") return;
      const pct = Math.min(34, Math.max(12, value));
      const cur = sidebarRef.current?.getSize();
      if (cur == null || Math.abs(cur - pct) > 0.05) {
        sidebarRef.current?.resize(pct);
      }
    });
  }, []);

  const handleNew = useCallback((projectId?: string | null) => {
    chat.newConversation(projectId);
    setMobileOpen(false);
  }, [chat]);

  const handleOpen = useCallback(
    (id: string) => {
      chat.openConversation(id);
      setMobileOpen(false);
    },
    [chat]
  );

  // ── Fork : crée une branche depuis un message et l'ouvre ────────────
  const handleFork = useCallback(
    async (messageId: string) => {
      const newId = await chat.forkConversation(messageId);
      if (newId) {
        toast({
          title: "Branche créée",
          description: "La conversation a été dupliquée depuis ce message.",
        });
        chat.openConversation(newId);
      } else {
        toast({ title: "Impossible de créer la branche", variant: "destructive" });
      }
    },
    [chat, toast]
  );

  // ── Artefacts : ouverture (panneau bureau / plein écran mobile) ─────
  const handleOpenArtifact = useCallback(
    (artifact: Artifact) => {
      chat.openArtifact(artifact);
      if (isMobile) setViewerOpen(true);
    },
    [chat, isMobile]
  );

  // ── Terminal du tour : activité outil du message qui porte l'artefact ─
  // Le stream vivant d'abord (étapes en direct), le message persisté ensuite.
  const resolveTurnTools = useCallback(
    (messageId?: string): TurnTools => {
      const stream = messageId ? streamStore.get().streamsRecord[messageId] : undefined;
      if (stream) return { events: stream.toolEvents, live: true };
      const msg = messageId
        ? chat.messages.find((m) => m.id === messageId)
        : undefined;
      return { events: msg?.toolEvents ?? [], live: false };
    },
    [chat.messages]
  );

  // ── Export de la conversation active ────────────────────────────────
  const handleExport = useCallback(
    (format: ExportFormat) => {
      if (!activeConversation || chat.messages.length === 0) return;
      try {
        if (format === "markdown") exportMarkdown(activeConversation, chat.messages);
        else if (format === "json") exportJson(activeConversation, chat.messages);
        else exportPdf(activeConversation, chat.messages);
        toast({
          title:
            format === "pdf"
              ? "Impression PDF lancée"
              : "Export téléchargé",
          description:
            format === "markdown"
              ? "Fichier Markdown généré."
              : format === "json"
                ? "Fichier JSON généré."
                : "Choisissez « Enregistrer au format PDF » dans la boîte d'impression.",
        });
      } catch {
        toast({ title: "Échec de l'export", variant: "destructive" });
      }
    },
    [activeConversation, chat.messages, toast]
  );

  // ── Raccourcis clavier globaux ─────────────────────────────────────
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      const mod = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;
      if (mod && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
        e.preventDefault();
        setPaletteOpen((o) => !o);
        return;
      }
      if (mod && e.shiftKey && e.key.toLowerCase() === "o") {
        e.preventDefault();
        handleNew();
        return;
      }
      if (
        e.key === "Escape" &&
        !paletteOpen
      ) {
        // Lot 5 (audit) : un dialog/sheet/popover Radix OUVERT consomme Échap —
        // ces overlays sont rendus en PORTAIL (le target n'est ni INPUT ni
        // TEXTAREA) : sans cette garde, fermer une fiche arrêtait aussi la
        // génération en cours. Un overlay vivant garde la main sur Échap.
        if (document.querySelector('[role="dialog"][data-state="open"], [data-radix-popper-content-wrapper]')) {
          return;
        }
        const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
        const inField =
          target &&
          (target.tagName === "TEXTAREA" ||
            target.tagName === "INPUT" ||
            target.isContentEditable);
        if (chat.panelOpen && !inField) {
          // Échap referme d'abord le panneau des artefacts
          e.preventDefault();
          chat.closePanel();
          return;
        }
        if (!inField && chat.isBusy) {
          e.preventDefault();
          chat.stopGeneration();
        }
      }
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    return () => window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
  }, [handleNew, paletteOpen, chat]);

  // ── Notifications de fin de génération (onglet en arrière-plan) ────
  const wasStreamingRef = useRef(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const streamingNow = chat.streaming !== null;
    if (streamingNow) {
      wasStreamingRef.current = true;
      if (document.hidden) setGeneratingTitle(true);
    } else if (wasStreamingRef.current) {
      wasStreamingRef.current = false;
      setGeneratingTitle(false);
      if (document.hidden) {
        playCompletionBeep();
        flashTitle("Réponse prête — Hermes Web");
      }
    }
  }, [chat.streaming]);

  // Titre dynamique pendant la génération (même onglet visible)
  useEffect(() => {
    const onVisibility = () => {
      if (document.hidden) {
        if (chat.streaming) setGeneratingTitle(true);
      } else {
        setGeneratingTitle(false);
      }
    };
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
  }, [chat.streaming]);

  const sidebar = (
    <Sidebar
      conversations={chat.conversations}
      projects={chat.projects}
      activeId={chat.activeId}
      mode={chat.mode}
      serverModel={chat.serverModel}
      engineProviders={chat.engineProviders}
      updateAvailable={chat.updateAvailable}
      connected={chat.connected}
      isBusy={chat.isBusy}
      onNew={handleNew}
      onOpen={handleOpen}
      onDelete={chat.deleteConversation}
      onRename={chat.renameConversation}
      onTogglePin={chat.togglePin}
      onSearchMessages={chat.searchMessages}
      onCreateProject={chat.createProject}
      onRenameProject={chat.renameProject}
      onDeleteProject={chat.deleteProject}
      onReorderProjects={chat.reorderProjects}
      onMoveConversation={chat.moveConversation}
      onOpenSettings={() => {
        setSettingsOpen(true);
        setMobileOpen(false); // mobile : le sheet remplace la sidebar
      }}
    />
  );

  return (
    // Wrapper à hauteur fixe : ResizablePanelGroup pose un style inline
    // height:100% qui écrase h-dvh — il faut un parent dimensionné, sinon
    // l'app tient sur la hauteur de son contenu (composer non ancré en bas,
    // bande vide sous la sidebar — constaté en QA 2026-11-14).
    <div className="h-app-full w-full bg-background text-foreground">
      <ResizablePanelGroup direction="horizontal" className="h-full w-full">
      {/* Sidebar bureau — largeur fluide, mémorisée */}
      {!isMobile ? (
        <>
          <ResizablePanel
            ref={sidebarRef}
            id="sidebar"
            order={1}
            defaultSize={19}
            minSize={14}
            maxSize={34}
            onResize={handleSidebarResize}
            className="min-w-0"
          >
            {sidebar}
          </ResizablePanel>
          <ResizableHandle className="bg-sidebar-border" />
        </>
      ) : null}

      {/* Zone de conversation (le chef d'orchestre) */}
      <ResizablePanel id="chat" order={2} defaultSize={81} minSize={36} className="min-w-0">
        <main className="flex h-full min-w-0 flex-col">
          <ChatHeader
            activeConversation={activeConversation}
            mode={chat.mode}
            connected={chat.connected}
            hasMessages={chat.messages.length > 0}
            hasPrompt={chat.promptInfo !== null}
            onOpenPrompt={() => setPromptOpen(true)}
            share={share}
            onShareCreate={handleShareCreate}
            onShareRevoke={handleShareRevoke}
            onExport={handleExport}
            onOpenSidebar={() => setMobileOpen(true)}
          />
          <MessageList
            messages={chat.messages}
            loadingHistory={chat.loadingHistory}
            isBusy={chat.isBusy}
            turnError={chat.turnError}
            stallNotice={chat.stallNotice}
            artifacts={chat.artifacts}
            artifactStreams={chat.streamingArtifacts}
            onSuggestion={(prompt) => chat.sendMessage(prompt)}
            onRegenerate={() => chat.regenerate()}
            onRegenerateVariant={(v) => chat.regenerate(v)}
            onSetVersion={chat.setMessageVersion}
            onRetryError={chat.retryAfterError}
            onDismissError={chat.dismissTurnError}
            onEdit={chat.editMessage}
            onDelete={chat.deleteMessage}
            onFork={handleFork}
            onOpenArtifact={handleOpenArtifact}
            onRetryArtifact={chat.retryArtifact}
            conversationMode={chat.conversationMode}
            modeFlowLive={chat.modeFlowLive}
          />
          <Composer
            disabled={!chat.connected}
            isBusy={chat.isBusy}
            draftKey={chat.activeId ?? "new"}
            models={chat.models}
            modelsSource={chat.modelsSource}
            mode={chat.mode}
            selectedModel={chat.selectedModel}
            onSelectModel={chat.setSelectedModel}
            conversationMode={chat.conversationMode}
            conversationModeConfig={chat.conversationModeConfig}
            onSetConversationMode={chat.setConversationMode}
            effort={chat.effort}
            onEffortChange={chat.setEffort}
            expert={chat.expert}
            onExpertChange={chat.setExpert}
            permissionsMode={chat.permissionsMode}
            onPermissionsChange={chat.setPermissionsMode}
            webSearch={chat.webSearch}
            onToggleWebSearch={chat.toggleWebSearch}
            webSearchAvailable={webSearchAvailable}
            contextBase={chat.contextBase}
            canCompact={chat.canCompact}
            compactRunning={chat.compacting}
            onCompact={chat.compactConversation}
            onNewSession={() => {
              void chat.startNewSession();
            }}
            onSend={(text, attachments) => chat.sendMessage(text, attachments)}
            onStop={chat.stopGeneration}
            onUploadFile={chat.uploadAttachment}
            onTranscribe={chat.transcribeAudio}
          />
        </main>
      </ResizablePanel>

      {/* Panneau artefact (bureau) — s'ouvre automatiquement à la naissance */}
      {chat.panelOpen && !isMobile ? (
        <>
          <ResizableHandle />
          <ResizablePanel
            id="artifacts"
            order={3}
            defaultSize={44}
            minSize={26}
            maxSize={62}
            className="min-w-0"
          >
            <ArtifactPanel
              artifacts={chat.artifacts}
              selectedId={chat.panelArtifactId}
              streamingArtifacts={chat.streamingArtifacts}
              resolveTurnTools={resolveTurnTools}
              onSelectVersion={chat.selectArtifactVersion}
              onClose={chat.closePanel}
              onRetry={chat.retryArtifact}
              fetchVersions={chat.fetchArtifactVersions}
            />
          </ResizablePanel>
        </>
      ) : null}

      {/* Sidebar mobile */}
      <Sheet open={mobileOpen} onOpenChange={setMobileOpen}>
        <SheetContent side="left" className="w-[300px] gap-0 p-0">
          <SheetTitle className="sr-only">Menu des conversations</SheetTitle>
          {sidebar}
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>

      {/* Visionneuse artefact plein écran (mobile) */}
      <ArtifactViewerDialog
        open={viewerOpen}
        onOpenChange={setViewerOpen}
        artifacts={chat.artifacts}
        selectedId={chat.panelArtifactId}
        streamingArtifacts={chat.streamingArtifacts}
        resolveTurnTools={resolveTurnTools}
        onSelectVersion={chat.selectArtifactVersion}
        onRetry={chat.retryArtifact}
        fetchVersions={chat.fetchArtifactVersions}
      />

      {/* Volet Paramètres v4 (860 px, rail) — Interface, Modèles,
          Fournisseurs, Clés de services, Extensions, Hermes, Outils,
          Skills, Profils, Système… */}
      <SettingsPanel
        open={settingsOpen}
        onOpenChange={setSettingsOpen}
        mode={chat.mode}
        engineProviders={chat.engineProviders}
        connected={chat.connected}
        models={chat.models}
        selectedModel={chat.selectedModel}
        projects={chat.projects}
        api={providersApi}
        promptsApi={promptsApi}
        memoryApi={memoryApi}
        serverVersion={chat.serverVersion}
        updateAvailable={chat.updateAvailable}
        appRevision={chat.serverRevision}
        chromeHeadless={chat.chromeHeadless}
        gatewayUrl={chat.gatewayUrl}
        gatewayConfigured={chat.gatewayConfigured}
        gatewayTokenSet={chat.gatewayTokenSet}
        onConnectGateway={chat.connectGateway}
        webSearchAvailable={webSearchAvailable}
      />

      {/* Sheet « Prompt système de la conversation » (F-11) */}
      <SystemPromptPanel
        open={promptOpen}
        onOpenChange={setPromptOpen}
        prompt={promptsApi.prompt}
        presets={promptsApi.presets}
        conversationTitle={activeConversation?.title ?? null}
        onSave={(content, presetTitle) => {
          void promptsApi.savePrompt(content, presetTitle);
        }}
        onClear={() => {
          void promptsApi.clearPrompt();
        }}
        onCreatePreset={promptsApi.createPreset}
      />

      {/* Palette de commandes (Cmd/Ctrl+K) */}
      <CommandPalette
        open={paletteOpen}
        onOpenChange={setPaletteOpen}
        conversations={chat.conversations}
        activeConversation={activeConversation}
        models={chat.models}
        selectedModel={chat.selectedModel}
        isBusy={chat.isBusy}
        hasMessages={chat.messages.length > 0}
        onNewConversation={handleNew}
        onSelectModel={chat.setSelectedModel}
        onOpenConversation={handleOpen}
        onTogglePin={chat.togglePin}
        onRegenerate={chat.regenerate}
        onStop={chat.stopGeneration}
        onExport={handleExport}
        onSearchMessages={chat.searchMessages}
      />
      </ResizablePanelGroup>
      {/* Pont de dépannage (F-31) — visible quand l'URL porte ?debug=1 :
          journal live + « Copier le diagnostic » à coller à l'agent. */}
      <DebugBridge />
    </div>
  );
}

// Route publique de partage (F-13) — /shared/<token>, lecture seule.
// Page serveur minimale : le composant client fetch le snapshot figé du
// mini-service. Accessible sans session, jamais indexée (robots noindex).
import type { Metadata } from "next";
import { SharedConversationView } from "@/components/shared/shared-conversation-view";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "Conversation partagée — Hermes Web",
  robots: { index: false, follow: false },
};

export default async function SharedConversationPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ token: string }>;
}) {
  const { token } = await params;
  return <SharedConversationView token={token} />;
}

================= FILE: src/components/chat/artifact-card.tsx =================
// Carte artefact inline dans le message : compacte, cliquable, vivante.
// États : génération (squelette + étapes, code qui streame dans la carte),
// rendu en cours (Chrome), prêt (vignette + actions), erreur (Réessayer).
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  Braces,
  Download,
  FileCode2,
  FileText,
  Globe,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Loader2,
  Package,
  RefreshCw,
  Sheet,
  Table,
  ExternalLink,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  artifactKindLabel,
  formatArtifactBytes,
  type Artifact,
  type ArtifactKind,
} from "@/lib/chat-types";
import { artifactUrl, downloadArtifact, openArtifactInTab } from "@/lib/artifact-utils";
import { VersionPopover } from "@/components/chat/version-popover";

function KindIcon({ kind, className }: { kind: ArtifactKind; className?: string }) {
  const Icon =
    kind === "html"
      ? Globe
      : kind === "svg" || kind === "image"
        ? ImageIcon
        : kind === "json"
          ? Braces
          : kind === "csv"
            ? Sheet
            : kind === "code"
              ? FileCode2
              : kind === "pdf"
                ? FileText
                : Package;
  return <Icon className={className} />;
}

const KIND_TINT: Record<ArtifactKind, string> = {
  html: "bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400",
  code: "bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400",
  markdown: "bg-teal-500/15 text-teal-600 dark:text-teal-400",
  csv: "bg-green-500/15 text-green-600 dark:text-green-400",
  json: "bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-600 dark:text-rose-400",
  svg: "bg-fuchsia-500/15 text-fuchsia-600 dark:text-fuchsia-400",
  image: "bg-fuchsia-500/15 text-fuchsia-600 dark:text-fuchsia-400",
  pdf: "bg-red-500/15 text-red-600 dark:text-red-400",
};

function timeLabel(iso: string): string {
  try {
    return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString("fr-FR", {
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
    });
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

/** Trois dernières lignes d'un source en cours de stream / terminé. */
function codePreviewTail(source: string, maxLines = 3): string {
  const lines = source.split("\n");
  const tail = lines.slice(-maxLines).join("\n");
  return tail.length > 260 ? tail.slice(-260) : tail;
}

interface ArtifactCardProps {
  artifact: Artifact;
  /** Contenu streamé en direct (génération en cours). */
  streamingContent?: string;
  onOpen: (artifact: Artifact) => void;
  onRetry: (artifactId: string) => void;
}

export function ArtifactCard({ artifact, streamingContent, onOpen, onRetry }: ArtifactCardProps) {
  const [imgError, setImgError] = useState(false);
  const isGenerating = artifact.status === "generating";
  const isRendering = artifact.status === "rendering";
  const isError = artifact.status === "error";
  const showStream =
    isGenerating && streamingContent !== undefined && streamingContent.length > 0;
  const hasThumb = !!artifact.thumbnailUrl && !imgError;

  return (
    <div
      className="group/art w-full max-w-[420px] overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border bg-card shadow-sm transition-all hover:border-hermes/50 hover:shadow-md"
      role="button"
      tabIndex={0}
      aria-label={`Artefact ${artifact.name} — ${artifactKindLabel(artifact.kind)}`}
      onClick={() => onOpen(artifact)}
      onKeyDown={(e) => {
        // C-04 : ne réagir qu'aux touches frappées sur la carte ELLE-MÊME —
        // les événements remontés depuis un Portal (popover de versions,
        // Input d'étiquette) ne doivent pas ouvrir l'artefact.
        if (e.target !== e.currentTarget) return;
        if (e.key === "Enter" || e.key === " ") {
          e.preventDefault();
          onOpen(artifact);
        }
      }}
    >
      {/* En-tête : icône, nom, version, statut */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 px-3 py-2.5">
        <span
          className={`flex h-9 w-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg ${KIND_TINT[artifact.kind]}`}
        >
          <KindIcon kind={artifact.kind} className="h-4.5 w-4.5" />
        </span>
        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
            <p className="truncate font-mono text-[12.5px] font-medium">{artifact.name}</p>
            {artifact.version > 1 ? (
              // F-26 : le badge EST la porte du journal des versions (design
              // validé : un seul point d'entrée, pas de bouton de plus).
              <VersionPopover artifact={artifact}>
                <Badge
                  variant="outline"
                  className="h-4 shrink-0 cursor-pointer rounded px-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-hermes-text transition-colors hover:border-hermes/60 hover:bg-hermes/10"
                  title="Historique des versions"
                >
                  v{artifact.version}
                </Badge>
              </VersionPopover>
            ) : null}
          </div>
          <p className="mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 truncate text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
            {isGenerating || isRendering ? (
              <>
                <Loader2 className="h-3 w-3 shrink-0 animate-spin text-hermes-text" />
                <span className="truncate">
                  {artifact.statusLabel ??
                    (isGenerating ? `Génération de ${artifact.name}…` : "Rendu…")}
                </span>
              </>
            ) : isError ? (
              <span className="truncate text-destructive">
                {artifact.error ?? "Échec de la génération"}
              </span>
            ) : (
              <>
                <span>{artifactKindLabel(artifact.kind)}</span>
                <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                <span>{formatArtifactBytes(artifact.size)}</span>
                {artifact.pdfSize ? (
                  <>
                    <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                    <span>PDF {formatArtifactBytes(artifact.pdfSize)}</span>
                  </>
                ) : null}
                <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
                <span>{timeLabel(artifact.createdAt)}</span>
              </>
            )}
          </p>
        </div>
        {/* Actions */}
        <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5" onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
          {isError ? (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg text-hermes-text"
              onClick={() => onRetry(artifact.id)}
              aria-label="Réessayer le rendu"
              title="Réessayer"
            >
              <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </Button>
          ) : artifact.status === "ready" ? (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
              onClick={() => openArtifactInTab(artifact)}
              aria-label={`Ouvrir ${artifact.name} dans un nouvel onglet`}
              title="Ouvrir dans un nouvel onglet"
            >
              <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </Button>
          ) : null}
          {!isError ? (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
              onClick={() =>
                downloadArtifact(artifact, artifact.kind === "html" ? "pdf" : "source")
              }
              aria-label={`Télécharger ${artifact.name}`}
              title={artifact.kind === "html" && artifact.pdfUrl ? "Télécharger le PDF" : "Télécharger"}
            >
              <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            </Button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Zone d'aperçu */}
      {isError ? (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-t border-border/60 bg-destructive/5 px-3 py-2 text-[12.5px] text-destructive">
          <AlertTriangle className="h-3.5 w-3.5 shrink-0" />
          <span className="truncate">{artifact.error ?? "Le rendu a échoué."}</span>
        </div>
      ) : hasThumb ? (
        <div className="relative max-h-24 overflow-hidden border-t border-border/60">
          <img
            src={artifactUrl(artifact.thumbnailUrl!)}
            alt={`Aperçu de ${artifact.name}`}
            className="h-24 w-full object-cover object-top"
            loading="lazy"
            onError={() => setImgError(true)}
          />
          {artifact.renderedBy ? (
            <span className="absolute bottom-1.5 right-1.5 rounded-md bg-background/85 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium text-muted-foreground backdrop-blur-sm">
              Rendu Chrome
            </span>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      ) : showStream ? (
        <div className="border-t border-border/60 bg-muted/40 px-3 py-2">
          <pre className="overflow-hidden font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
            <code>{codePreviewTail(streamingContent!)}</code>
          </pre>
        </div>
      ) : isGenerating || isRendering ? (
        <div className="border-t border-border/60">
          <div className="flex h-16 items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-muted/30 text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground">
            {isRendering && artifact.kind === "html" ? (
              <>
                <Table className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-hermes-text" />
                {artifact.statusLabel ?? "Rendu de l'aperçu (Chrome)…"}
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <span className="flex gap-1" aria-hidden="true">
                  <span className="typing-dot h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-hermes" />
                  <span className="typing-dot h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-hermes" />
                  <span className="typing-dot h-1.5 w-1.5 rounded-full bg-hermes" />
                </span>
                {artifact.statusLabel ?? `Génération de ${artifact.name}…`}
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : artifact.preview ? (
        <div className="border-t border-border/60 bg-muted/30 px-3 py-1.5">
          <pre className="overflow-hidden font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed text-muted-foreground">
            <code>
              {artifact.preview.split("\n").slice(0, 3).join("\n").slice(0, 220)}
            </code>
          </pre>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

================= FILE: src/components/chat/artifact-panel.tsx =================
// Panneau artefact (bureau, split view) + visionneuse plein écran (mobile).
// Un rendu par type : code (coloration + numéros + wrap + diff), image/SVG
// (zoom), HTML (aperçu = PNG plein page rendu par Chrome / code + export PDF),
// Markdown, CSV (tableau), JSON, PDF (visionneuse intégrée).
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  Check,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Copy,
  Download,
  ExternalLink,
  FileDown,
  GitCompare,
  Globe,
  Loader2,
  Maximize2,
  Minimize2,
  PanelRightClose,
  RefreshCw,
  WrapText,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Badge } from "@/components/ui/badge";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuSeparator,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from "@/components/ui/dropdown-menu";
import { MarkdownContent } from "@/components/chat/markdown-content";
import {
  artifactKindLabel,
  formatArtifactBytes,
  type Artifact,
  type TurnTools,
} from "@/lib/chat-types";
import {
  artifactUrl,
  downloadArtifact,
  openArtifactInTab,
} from "@/lib/artifact-utils";
import {
  useArtifactSource,
  CodeView,
  DiffView,
  CsvTableView,
  JsonView,
  ImageView,
  LiveSourceView,
} from "@/components/chat/artifact-views";
import { ArtifactTerminal } from "@/components/chat/artifact-terminal";
import { serviceFetchInit } from "@/lib/service";
import { useStreamTurnTools } from "@/lib/stream-store";

// ── Encart terminal du tour porteur (F-29, régression §60) ───────────────

/** Terminal des outils du tour qui porte l'artefact : abonné au store de
 *  stream — il suit chaque stream:tool EN DIRECT pendant le tour, sans
 *  faire re-rendre page.tsx (qui ne s'abonne plus au registre depuis le
 *  bonus perf §60 : resolveTurnTools y lisait le store sans abonnement,
 *  le terminal restait figé). Repli sur la résolution synchrone pour les
 *  messages persistés d'un tour terminé. */
function ArtifactTurnTerminal({
  messageId,
  resolveTurnTools,
}: {
  messageId?: string;
  resolveTurnTools: (messageId?: string) => TurnTools;
}) {
  const live = useStreamTurnTools(messageId);
  const tools = live ?? resolveTurnTools(messageId);
  return <ArtifactTerminal key={messageId} {...tools} />;
}

// ── Chargement du source (cache module partagé panneau / visionneuse) ───

// ── Corps du panneau : un rendu par type ─────────────────────────────────

type ViewMode = "preview" | "code";

function ArtifactBody({
  artifact,
  source,
  streamingContent,
  versions,
  onRetry,
  fetchVersions,
}: {
  artifact: Artifact;
  source: string | null;
  streamingContent?: string;
  versions: Artifact[];
  onRetry: (id: string) => void;
  fetchVersions: (key: string) => Promise<Artifact[]>;
}) {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<ViewMode>("preview");
  const [wrap, setWrap] = useState(false);
  const [diff, setDiff] = useState(false);
  const [prevSource, setPrevSource] = useState<string | null>(null);
  // Reset des vues quand on change d'artefact (état dérivé, pattern React)
  const [viewedKey, setViewedKey] = useState(artifact.key);
  const [viewedVersion, setViewedVersion] = useState(artifact.version);
  if (viewedKey !== artifact.key) {
    setViewedKey(artifact.key);
    setViewedVersion(artifact.version);
    setMode("preview");
    setDiff(false);
    setPrevSource(null);
  } else if (viewedVersion !== artifact.version) {
    setViewedVersion(artifact.version);
    setDiff(false);
    setPrevSource(null);
  }
  const hasPreviewToggle =
    artifact.kind === "html" ||
    artifact.kind === "svg" ||
    artifact.kind === "markdown" ||
    artifact.kind === "csv" ||
    artifact.kind === "json";
  const isLive = artifact.status === "generating" || artifact.status === "rendering";
  const canDiff =
    (artifact.kind === "code" || artifact.kind === "markdown") && artifact.version > 1;
  const prevArtifact = useMemo(
    () => versions.find((v) => v.version === artifact.version - 1) ?? null,
    [versions, artifact.version]
  );

  // Source de la version précédente quand le diff est activé
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!diff || !prevArtifact) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(artifactUrl(prevArtifact.rawUrl), serviceFetchInit())
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.text() : ""))
      .then((t) => {
        if (!cancelled) setPrevSource(t);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (!cancelled) setPrevSource("");
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [diff, prevArtifact]);

  // ── Génération / rendu en cours ────────────────────────────────────
  if (isLive) {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-border/60 bg-muted/30 px-3 py-2 text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground">
          <Loader2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5 animate-spin text-hermes-text" />
          {artifact.statusLabel ??
            (artifact.status === "generating"
              ? `Génération de ${artifact.name}…`
              : "Rendu de l'aperçu (Chrome)…")}
        </div>
        {streamingContent && streamingContent.length > 0 ? (
          // Perf 2026-11-19 : texte brut pendant la génération — l'ancien
          // CodeView (Prism) re-tokenisait TOUT le fichier à chaque delta
          // (~60 Hz). La colorisation n'arrive qu'à l'état « ready ».
          <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
            <LiveSourceView source={streamingContent} />
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center p-8">
            <div className="w-full max-w-sm space-y-2">
              <div className="h-3 w-3/4 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
              <div className="h-3 w-full animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
              <div className="h-3 w-5/6 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
              <div className="h-3 w-2/3 animate-pulse rounded bg-muted" />
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    );
  }

  if (artifact.status === "error") {
    return (
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-3 p-8 text-center">
        <AlertTriangle className="h-8 w-8 text-destructive" />
        <div>
          <p className="font-medium">Le rendu a échoué</p>
          <p className="mt-1 max-w-sm text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground">
            {artifact.error ?? "Erreur inconnue pendant la génération de l'artefact."}
          </p>
        </div>
        {artifact.kind === "html" ? (
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            className="gap-2 rounded-lg"
            onClick={() => onRetry(artifact.id)}
          >
            <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Réessayer le rendu
          </Button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
    );
  }

  // ── Prêt : rendu par type ──────────────────────────────────────────
  const toggle = hasPreviewToggle ? (
    <div className="flex items-center rounded-lg border border-border bg-muted/40 p-0.5">
      {(["preview", "code"] as const).map((m) => (
        <button
          key={m}
          onClick={() => setMode(m)}
          className={`rounded-md px-2.5 py-1 text-[12.5px] font-medium transition-colors ${
            mode === m
              ? "bg-background text-foreground shadow-sm"
              : "text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
          }`}
          aria-pressed={mode === m}
        >
          {m === "preview" ? "Aperçu" : "Code"}
        </button>
      ))}
    </div>
  ) : null;

  let body: React.ReactNode = null;
  if (artifact.kind === "html") {
    body =
      mode === "preview" ? (
        artifact.thumbnailUrl ? (
          <ImageView src={artifactUrl(artifact.thumbnailUrl)} alt={`Aperçu de ${artifact.name}`} />
        ) : (
          <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center p-8 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            Aperçu indisponible.
          </div>
        )
      ) : (
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
          <CodeView source={source ?? ""} language="html" wrap={wrap} />
        </div>
      );
  } else if (artifact.kind === "svg") {
    body =
      mode === "preview" ? (
        <ImageView src={artifactUrl(artifact.rawUrl)} alt={`Aperçu de ${artifact.name}`} />
      ) : (
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
          <CodeView source={source ?? ""} language="xml" wrap={wrap} />
        </div>
      );
  } else if (artifact.kind === "image") {
    body = <ImageView src={artifactUrl(artifact.rawUrl)} alt={artifact.name} />;
  } else if (artifact.kind === "pdf") {
    body = (
      <iframe
        src={artifactUrl(artifact.rawUrl)}
        title={`PDF ${artifact.name}`}
        className="min-h-0 flex-1 border-0 bg-muted/30"
      />
    );
  } else if (artifact.kind === "markdown") {
    body =
      mode === "preview" ? (
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto px-4 py-3">
          <MarkdownContent content={source ?? ""} />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
          <CodeView source={source ?? ""} language="markdown" wrap={wrap} />
        </div>
      );
  } else if (artifact.kind === "csv") {
    body =
      mode === "preview" ? (
        <CsvTableView source={source ?? ""} />
      ) : (
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
          <CodeView source={source ?? ""} language="text" wrap={wrap} />
        </div>
      );
  } else if (artifact.kind === "json") {
    body =
      mode === "preview" ? (
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
          <JsonView source={source ?? ""} />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
          <CodeView source={source ?? ""} language="text" wrap={wrap} />
        </div>
      );
  } else {
    // code
    body = diff && prevSource !== null ? (
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
        <DiffView oldSource={prevSource} newSource={source ?? ""} />
      </div>
    ) : (
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto">
        <CodeView
          source={source ?? ""}
          language={artifact.language}
          wrap={wrap}
        />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
      <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 border-b border-border/60 px-3 py-2">
        {toggle}
        {artifact.kind === "code" || artifact.kind === "html" || artifact.kind === "markdown" ||
        artifact.kind === "csv" || artifact.kind === "json" || artifact.kind === "svg" ? (
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="icon"
            className={`h-7 w-7 rounded-lg ${wrap ? "text-hermes-text" : "text-muted-foreground"}`}
            onClick={() => setWrap((w) => !w)}
            aria-label="Retour à la ligne"
            title="Retour à la ligne on/off"
          >
            <WrapText className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        {canDiff ? (
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="sm"
            className={`h-7 gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-2 text-[12.5px] ${
              diff ? "text-hermes-text" : "text-muted-foreground"
            }`}
            onClick={() => setDiff((d) => !d)}
            aria-pressed={diff}
            title={`Différences v${artifact.version - 1} → v${artifact.version}`}
          >
            <GitCompare className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            Diff v{artifact.version - 1}→v{artifact.version}
          </Button>
        ) : null}
        {artifact.kind === "html" && artifact.pdfUrl ? (
          <Button
            variant="outline"
            size="sm"
            className="ml-auto h-7 gap-1.5 rounded-lg border-hermes/40 px-2.5 text-[12.5px] text-hermes-text hover:bg-hermes/10"
            onClick={() => downloadArtifact(artifact, "pdf")}
          >
            <FileDown className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
            PDF {artifact.pdfSize ? formatArtifactBytes(artifact.pdfSize) : ""}
          </Button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      {body}
    </div>
  );
}

// ── En-tête partagé : nom, version, actions ──────────────────────────────

function useCopySource(source: string | null) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false);
  const copy = useCallback(async () => {
    if (source === null) return;
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(source);
      setCopied(true);
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 1600);
    } catch {
      /* clipboard indisponible */
    }
  }, [source]);
  return { copied, copy };
}

function ArtifactHeaderActions({
  artifact,
  source,
  versions,
  onRetry,
  onSelectVersion,
}: {
  artifact: Artifact;
  source: string | null;
  versions: Artifact[];
  onRetry: (id: string) => void;
  onSelectVersion: (artifact: Artifact) => void;
}) {
  const { copied, copy } = useCopySource(source);
  const canDownload = artifact.status === "ready";
  return (
    <div className="flex shrink-0 items-center gap-0.5">
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon"
        className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
        onClick={() => openArtifactInTab(artifact)}
        disabled={!canDownload}
        aria-label="Ouvrir dans un nouvel onglet"
        title="Ouvrir dans un nouvel onglet"
      >
        <ExternalLink className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
      </Button>
      {versions.length > 1 ? (
        <DropdownMenu>
          <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="sm"
              className="h-7 gap-1 rounded-lg px-2 font-mono text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground"
              aria-label="Choisir la version"
              title="Versions"
            >
              v{artifact.version}
              <Badge variant="outline" className="h-4 rounded px-1 text-[11px]">
                {versions.length}
              </Badge>
            </Button>
          </DropdownMenuTrigger>
          <DropdownMenuContent align="end" className="min-w-44">
            <DropdownMenuLabel>Versions</DropdownMenuLabel>
            <DropdownMenuSeparator />
            {[...versions].reverse().map((v) => (
              <DropdownMenuItem
                key={v.id}
                onClick={() => onSelectVersion(v)}
                className={v.id === artifact.id ? "bg-muted font-medium" : ""}
              >
                <span className="font-mono text-[12.5px]">v{v.version}</span>
                <span className="ml-2 text-[12.5px] text-muted-foreground">
                  {new Date(v.createdAt).toLocaleString("fr-FR", {
                    day: "2-digit",
                    month: "short",
                    hour: "2-digit",
                    minute: "2-digit",
                  })}
                </span>
                {v.status === "error" ? (
                  <AlertTriangle className="ml-auto h-3 w-3 text-destructive" />
                ) : null}
              </DropdownMenuItem>
            ))}
          </DropdownMenuContent>
        </DropdownMenu>
      ) : null}
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon"
        className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
        onClick={copy}
        disabled={source === null}
        aria-label="Copier le source"
        title="Copier le source"
      >
        {copied ? <Check className="h-3.5 w-3.5 text-emerald-500" /> : <Copy className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
      </Button>
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon"
        className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
        onClick={() => downloadArtifact(artifact, artifact.kind === "html" ? "pdf" : "source")}
        disabled={!canDownload}
        aria-label="Télécharger"
        title={
          artifact.kind === "html" && artifact.pdfUrl
            ? "Télécharger le PDF vectoriel"
            : "Télécharger le fichier"
        }
      >
        <Download className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
      </Button>
      {artifact.status === "error" && artifact.kind === "html" ? (
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="icon"
          className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg text-hermes-text"
          onClick={() => onRetry(artifact.id)}
          aria-label="Réessayer"
          title="Réessayer le rendu"
        >
          <RefreshCw className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />
        </Button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

function ArtifactMetaLine({ artifact }: { artifact: Artifact }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex shrink-0 flex-wrap items-center gap-x-2 gap-y-1 border-t border-border/60 px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
      <span>{artifactKindLabel(artifact.kind)}</span>
      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
      <span>{formatArtifactBytes(artifact.size)}</span>
      {artifact.pdfSize ? (
        <>
          <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
          <span>PDF {formatArtifactBytes(artifact.pdfSize)}</span>
        </>
      ) : null}
      <span aria-hidden="true">·</span>
      <span>
        {new Date(artifact.createdAt).toLocaleString("fr-FR", {
          day: "2-digit",
          month: "short",
          hour: "2-digit",
          minute: "2-digit",
        })}
      </span>
      {artifact.renderedBy ? (
        <span className="ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md bg-muted px-1.5 py-0.5 font-medium">
          <Globe className="h-3 w-3 text-hermes-text" />
          Rendu Chrome — fidélité garantie
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Panneau bureau (split view) ──────────────────────────────────────────

export interface ArtifactPanelProps {
  artifacts: Artifact[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  streamingArtifacts: Record<string, string>;
  /** Activité outil du tour qui porte un artefact (encart terminal). */
  resolveTurnTools: (messageId?: string) => TurnTools;
  onSelectVersion: (artifact: Artifact) => void;
  onClose: () => void;
  onRetry: (artifactId: string) => void;
  fetchVersions: (key: string) => Promise<Artifact[]>;
}

export function ArtifactPanel({
  artifacts,
  selectedId,
  streamingArtifacts,
  resolveTurnTools,
  onSelectVersion,
  onClose,
  onRetry,
  fetchVersions,
}: ArtifactPanelProps) {
  // Onglets : dernière version de chaque clé, par ordre de création
  const tabs = useMemo(() => {
    const byKey = new Map<string, Artifact>();
    for (const a of artifacts) {
      const cur = byKey.get(a.key);
      if (!cur || a.version > cur.version) byKey.set(a.key, a);
    }
    return [...byKey.values()].sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));
  }, [artifacts]);

  const selected = useMemo(() => {
    const exact = artifacts.find((a) => a.id === selectedId);
    if (exact) return exact;
    const key = tabs.find((t) => t.id === selectedId)?.key;
    if (key) {
      const latest = artifacts.filter((a) => a.key === key).sort((a, b) => b.version - a.version);
      return latest[0] ?? null;
    }
    return tabs[0] ?? null;
  }, [artifacts, selectedId, tabs]);

  const versions = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selected) return [];
    return artifacts.filter((a) => a.key === selected.key).sort((a, b) => a.version - b.version);
  }, [artifacts, selected]);

  const source = useArtifactSource(selected);
  const streamingContent = selected ? streamingArtifacts[selected.key] : undefined;
  const selectedKey = selected?.key;
  const selectedHasVersions = !!selected && selected.version > 1;

  // Charge l'historique des versions d'une clé multi-versions (pour le diff)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedHasVersions && selectedKey) void fetchVersions(selectedKey);
  }, [selectedKey, selectedHasVersions, fetchVersions]);

  if (!selected) {
    return (
      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
        Aucun artefact dans cette conversation.
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-w-0 flex-col bg-background">
      {/* Onglets + actions */}
      <div className="flex h-12 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 border-b border-border/60 px-2">
        <div
          className="flex min-w-0 flex-1 items-center gap-1 overflow-x-auto"
          role="tablist"
          aria-label="Artefacts de la conversation"
        >
          {tabs.map((t) => (
            <button
              key={t.key}
              role="tab"
              aria-selected={t.key === selected.key}
              onClick={() => onSelectVersion(t)}
              className={`flex shrink-0 items-center gap-1.5 rounded-lg px-2.5 py-1.5 text-[12.5px] transition-colors ${
                t.key === selected.key
                  ? "bg-muted font-medium text-foreground"
                  : "text-muted-foreground hover:bg-muted/50 hover:text-foreground"
              }`}
              title={t.name}
            >
              <span className="max-w-36 truncate font-mono">{t.name}</span>
              {t.version > 1 ? (
                <span className="rounded bg-hermes/15 px-1 font-mono text-[11px] text-hermes-text">
                  v{t.version}
                </span>
              ) : null}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
        <ArtifactHeaderActions
          artifact={selected}
          source={source}
          versions={versions}
          onRetry={onRetry}
          onSelectVersion={onSelectVersion}
        />
        <Button
          variant="ghost"
          size="icon"
          className="h-7 w-7 shrink-0 rounded-lg text-muted-foreground hover:text-foreground"
          onClick={onClose}
          aria-label="Fermer le panneau des artefacts"
          title="Fermer"
        >
          <PanelRightClose className="h-4 w-4" />
        </Button>
      </div>

      <ArtifactBody
        artifact={selected}
        source={source}
        streamingContent={streamingContent}
        versions={versions}
        onRetry={onRetry}
        fetchVersions={fetchVersions}
      />
      <ArtifactTurnTerminal
        messageId={selected.messageId}
        resolveTurnTools={resolveTurnTools}
      />
      <ArtifactMetaLine artifact={selected} />
    </div>
  );
}

// ── Visionneuse plein écran (mobile) ─────────────────────────────────────

export function ArtifactViewerDialog({
  open,
  onOpenChange,
  artifacts,
  selectedId,
  streamingArtifacts,
  resolveTurnTools,
  onSelectVersion,
  onRetry,
  fetchVersions,
}: {
  open: boolean;
  onOpenChange: (open: boolean) => void;
  artifacts: Artifact[];
  selectedId: string | null;
  streamingArtifacts: Record<string, string>;
  /** Activité outil du tour qui porte un artefact (encart terminal). */
  resolveTurnTools: (messageId?: string) => TurnTools;
  onSelectVersion: (artifact: Artifact) => void;
  onRetry: (artifactId: string) => void;
  fetchVersions: (key: string) => Promise<Artifact[]>;
}) {
  const tabs = useMemo(() => {
    const byKey = new Map<string, Artifact>();
    for (const a of artifacts) {
      const cur = byKey.get(a.key);
      if (!cur || a.version > cur.version) byKey.set(a.key, a);
    }
    return [...byKey.values()].sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));
  }, [artifacts]);

  const selected = useMemo(
    () => artifacts.find((a) => a.id === selectedId) ?? tabs[0] ?? null,
    [artifacts, selectedId, tabs]
  );
  const idx = selected ? tabs.findIndex((t) => t.key === selected.key) : -1;

  const go = useCallback(
    (dir: 1 | -1) => {
      if (idx < 0 || tabs.length === 0) return;
      const next = tabs[(idx + dir + tabs.length) % tabs.length];
      onSelectVersion(next);
    },
    [idx, tabs, onSelectVersion]
  );

  // Swipe horizontal entre artefacts
  const touchX = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const onTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    touchX.current = e.touches[0]?.clientX ?? null;
  };
  const onTouchEnd = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    if (touchX.current === null) return;
    const dx = (e.changedTouches[0]?.clientX ?? touchX.current) - touchX.current;
    touchX.current = null;
    if (Math.abs(dx) > 60) go(dx < 0 ? 1 : -1);
  };

  const versions = useMemo(() => {
    if (!selected) return [];
    return artifacts.filter((a) => a.key === selected.key).sort((a, b) => a.version - b.version);
  }, [artifacts, selected]);
  const source = useArtifactSource(selected);
  const streamingContent = selected ? streamingArtifacts[selected.key] : undefined;

  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={onOpenChange}>
      <DialogContent
        className="flex h-[100dvh] max-h-[100dvh] w-full max-w-none flex-col gap-0 rounded-none border-0 p-0 sm:h-[100dvh] sm:max-h-[100dvh] sm:w-full sm:max-w-none sm:rounded-none sm:border-0"
        aria-describedby={undefined}
      >
        <DialogTitle className="sr-only">
          {selected ? `Artefact ${selected.name}` : "Artefact"}
        </DialogTitle>
        <div className="flex h-14 shrink-0 items-center gap-1 border-b border-border/60 px-2">
          {tabs.length > 1 ? (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              className="h-8 w-8 shrink-0 rounded-lg"
              onClick={() => go(-1)}
              aria-label="Artefact précédent"
            >
              <ChevronLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          ) : null}
          <div className="min-w-0 flex-1 text-center">
            <p className="truncate font-mono text-[12.5px] font-medium">
              {selected?.name ?? "—"}
            </p>
            {tabs.length > 1 ? (
              <p className="text-[11px] text-muted-foreground">
                {idx + 1} / {tabs.length} · glissez pour naviguer
              </p>
            ) : null}
          </div>
          {tabs.length > 1 ? (
            <Button
              variant="ghost"
              size="icon"
              className="h-8 w-8 shrink-0 rounded-lg"
              onClick={() => go(1)}
              aria-label="Artefact suivant"
            >
              <ChevronRight className="h-4 w-4" />
            </Button>
          ) : null}
          {selected ? (
            <ArtifactHeaderActions
              artifact={selected}
              source={source}
              versions={versions}
              onRetry={onRetry}
              onSelectVersion={onSelectVersion}
            />
          ) : null}
          <Button
            variant="ghost"
            size="icon"
            className="h-8 w-8 shrink-0 rounded-lg"
            onClick={() => onOpenChange(false)}
            aria-label="Fermer la visionneuse"
          >
            <X className="h-4 w-4" />
          </Button>
        </div>
        <div
          className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col"
          onTouchStart={onTouchStart}
          onTouchEnd={onTouchEnd}
        >
          {selected ? (
            <ArtifactBody
              artifact={selected}
              source={source}
              streamingContent={streamingContent}
              versions={versions}
              onRetry={onRetry}
              fetchVersions={fetchVersions}
            />
          ) : (
            <div className="flex flex-1 items-center justify-center text-sm text-muted-foreground">
              Aucun artefact.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        {selected ? (
          <ArtifactTurnTerminal
            messageId={selected.messageId}
            resolveTurnTools={resolveTurnTools}
          />
        ) : null}
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

================= FILE: src/components/chat/artifact-terminal.tsx =================
// Encart terminal du panneau artefacts : l'activité outil du tour qui a produit
// l'artefact affiché. Barre fine repliée par défaut — un tour vivant l'ouvre
// seul (un repli manuel est respecté) ; sans activité, pas d'encart du tout.
"use client";

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  AlertTriangle,
  Check,
  ChevronDown,
  Loader2,
  Terminal,
} from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import type { ToolEvent } from "@/lib/chat-types";

function heure(iso: string): string {
  try {
    return new Date(iso).toLocaleTimeString("fr-FR", {
      hour: "2-digit",
      minute: "2-digit",
      second: "2-digit",
    });
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
}

function LigneOutil({ event }: { event: ToolEvent }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start gap-2 px-3 py-1 font-mono text-[11px] leading-relaxed">
      {event.status === "running" ? (
        <Loader2 className="mt-0.5 h-3 w-3 shrink-0 animate-spin text-zinc-400" />
      ) : event.status === "error" ? (
        <AlertTriangle className="mt-0.5 h-3 w-3 shrink-0 text-red-400" />
      ) : (
        <Check className="mt-0.5 h-3 w-3 shrink-0 text-emerald-400" />
      )}
      <span className="shrink-0 text-zinc-500">{heure(event.at)}</span>
      <span className="shrink-0 text-zinc-200">{event.name}</span>
      {event.detail ? (
        <span className="min-w-0 flex-1 break-words whitespace-pre-wrap text-zinc-400">
          {event.detail}
        </span>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

export function ArtifactTerminal({
  events,
  live,
}: {
  events: ToolEvent[];
  live: boolean;
}) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  /** L'utilisateur a replié lui-même : ne pas rouvrir tant que le tour vit. */
  const [repliManuel, setRepliManuel] = useState(false);
  /** Mémorise le passage du tour en vivant (ajustement pendant le rendu). */
  const [vuVivant, setVuVivant] = useState(false);
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  // Un tour vivant ouvre l'encart tout seul — le tour terminé ne le bouge plus.
  // (idiome React « état dérivé pendant le rendu », comme le panneau voisin)
  if (live && !vuVivant) {
    setVuVivant(true);
    if (!repliManuel) setOpen(true);
  }

  // Défilement collé en bas à chaque nouvelle étape.
  useEffect(() => {
    const el = scrollRef.current;
    if (el && open) el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight;
  }, [events.length, open]);

  if (events.length === 0 && !live) return null;

  return (
    <div className="shrink-0 border-t border-border/60">
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        onClick={() => {
          setRepliManuel(true);
          setOpen((o) => !o);
        }}
        className="flex h-8 w-full items-center gap-2 bg-muted/30 px-3 text-[12.5px] font-normal text-muted-foreground transition-colors hover:bg-muted/50 hover:text-muted-foreground"
        aria-expanded={open}
        title={open ? "Replier le terminal" : "Déplier le terminal"}
      >
        <Terminal className="size-3.5 shrink-0 text-hermes-text" />
        <span>Terminal</span>
        {live ? (
          <span
            className="h-1.5 w-1.5 shrink-0 animate-pulse rounded-full bg-emerald-500"
            aria-hidden="true"
          />
        ) : (
          <span className="text-[11px]">
            {events.length} étape{events.length > 1 ? "s" : ""}
          </span>
        )}
        <ChevronDown
          className={`ml-auto size-3.5 shrink-0 transition-transform ${
            open ? "rotate-180" : ""
          }`}
        />
      </Button>
      {open ? (
        <div ref={scrollRef} className="max-h-44 overflow-y-auto bg-[#0d1117]">
          {events.length === 0 ? (
            <p className="px-3 py-2 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
              Hermes travaille…
            </p>
          ) : (
            events.map((ev, i) => (
              <LigneOutil key={`${ev.at}-${ev.name}-${i}`} event={ev} />
            ))
          )}
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}

================= FILE: src/components/chat/artifact-views.tsx =================
// Vues par type d'artefact + cache du source — extraits de
// artifact-panel.tsx (découpage 2026-11, FORK.md §10). Rendus PURS par type :
// code (coloration + numéros + wrap), diff ligne à ligne, tableau CSV, JSON,
// image/SVG avec zoom. Le cache borné et useArtifactSource (C-02) suivent :
// le panneau et la visionneuse plein écran partagent le même module.
"use client";

import { memo, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Loader2, Maximize2, Minimize2 } from "lucide-react";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { type Artifact } from "@/lib/chat-types";
import { artifactUrl, diffLines, parseCsv } from "@/lib/artifact-utils";
import { serviceFetchInit } from "@/lib/service";
import { useHighlightedCode, CODE_CONTAINER_STYLE, CODE_FONT_FAMILY } from "@/lib/highlight";

// ── Chargement du source (cache module partagé panneau / visionneuse) ───

// Cache borné (Lot 5, audit) : une conversation longue remplissait la carte
// sans limite ; les ÉCHECS de fetch ne sont plus mis en cache — un source
// qui a raté peut être rechargé (redimensionnement, retour d'onglet…).
const SOURCE_CACHE_MAX = 50;
const sourceCache = new Map<string, string>();

function cacheSource(id: string, text: string | null): void {
  if (text === null) {
    sourceCache.delete(id);
    return;
  }
  if (sourceCache.size >= SOURCE_CACHE_MAX && !sourceCache.has(id)) {
    const oldest = sourceCache.keys().next().value;
    if (oldest !== undefined) sourceCache.delete(oldest);
  }
  sourceCache.set(id, text);
}

export function useArtifactSource(artifact: Artifact | null): string | null {
  const [, forceRender] = useState(0);
  const artifactId = artifact?.id ?? null;
  const rawUrl = artifact?.rawUrl ?? null;
  const binaryKind = artifact?.kind === "image" || artifact?.kind === "pdf";
  // Un artefact en génération/rendering a un contenu vide ou PARTIEL en
  // base : le fetcher tant qu'il n'est pas "ready" reviendrait à figer le
  // cache sur une chaîne vide/tronquée (C-02) — et purge l'éventuel cache
  // obsolète au passage.
  const ready = artifact?.status === "ready" || artifact?.status === "error";
  const fetchable = Boolean(artifactId && rawUrl && !binaryKind && ready);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!artifactId) return;
    if (!ready) {
      // Règle C-02 : purge du cache tant que l'artefact n'est pas terminé —
      // l'id reste stable entre artifact:created et artifact:updated, un
      // cache précoce masquerait le contenu final jusqu'au rechargement.
      cacheSource(artifactId, null);
      return;
    }
    if (!rawUrl || binaryKind) return;
    if (sourceCache.has(artifactId)) return;
    let cancelled = false;
    fetch(artifactUrl(rawUrl), serviceFetchInit())
      .then((r) => (r.ok ? r.text() : Promise.reject(new Error(String(r.status)))))
      .then((text) => {
        cacheSource(artifactId, text);
        if (!cancelled) forceRender((n) => n + 1);
      })
      .catch(() => {
        cacheSource(artifactId, null); // pas de cache d'échec : recharge possible
        if (!cancelled) forceRender((n) => n + 1);
      });
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [artifactId, rawUrl, binaryKind, ready, fetchable]);

  // null = absent ou en erreur (rechargeable) ; "" = source vide lu avec succès.
  if (!artifactId) return null;
  const cached = sourceCache.get(artifactId);
  return cached === undefined ? null : cached;
}

// ── Vues par type ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

// Perf 2026-11-19 : CodeView migré de react-syntax-highlighter (Prism) vers
// shiki (src/lib/highlight.ts). Numéros de ligne en colonne CSS côté
// conteneur (plus de <span> par ligne générés par la lib). Mémoïsé : changer
// d'onglet ou re-rendre le panneau ne re-colorise jamais un source inchangé.
export const CodeView = memo(function CodeView({
  source,
  language,
  showLineNumbers = true,
  wrap = false,
  maxHeight,
}: {
  source: string;
  language?: string;
  showLineNumbers?: boolean;
  wrap?: boolean;
  maxHeight?: string;
}) {
  const code = source.replace(/\n$/, "");
  const html = useHighlightedCode(code, language);
  const lineCount = useMemo(() => code.split("\n").length, [code]);

  // Repli texte brut (premier rendu, grammaire absente) : mêmes couleurs,
  // mêmes numéros — la colonne CSS ci-dessous est partagée par les deux états.
  const gutter = showLineNumbers ? (
    <div
      aria-hidden="true"
      className="shrink-0 select-none border-r border-zinc-800 bg-[#161b22] px-2 py-[0.85rem] text-right text-zinc-600"
      style={{ fontFamily: CODE_FONT_FAMILY, fontSize: "12.5px", lineHeight: "1.65" }}
    >
      {Array.from({ length: lineCount }, (_, i) => (
        <div key={i}>{i + 1}</div>
      ))}
    </div>
  ) : null;

  return (
    <div
      className="flex min-h-full"
      style={{ ...CODE_CONTAINER_STYLE, ...(maxHeight ? { maxHeight } : {}) }}
    >
      {gutter}
      {html ? (
        <div
          className={`min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-x-auto [&_pre]:!my-0 [&_pre]:!py-[0.85rem] [&_pre]:px-[1rem] [&_pre]:!bg-transparent [&_code]:!font-[var(--font-geist-mono),ui-monospace,monospace] ${
            wrap ? "[&_pre]:!whitespace-pre-wrap [&_pre]:!break-words" : ""
          }`}
          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{ __html: html }}
        />
      ) : (
        <pre
          className={`min-w-0 flex-1 overflow-x-auto py-[0.85rem] pl-[1rem] ${
            wrap ? "whitespace-pre-wrap break-words" : ""
          }`}
          style={{ fontFamily: CODE_FONT_FAMILY }}
        >
          <code>{code}</code>
        </pre>
      )}
    </div>
  );
});

export function DiffView({ oldSource, newSource }: { oldSource: string; newSource: string }) {
  const rows = useMemo(() => diffLines(oldSource, newSource), [oldSource, newSource]);
  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    let add = 0;
    let del = 0;
    for (const r of rows) {
      if (r.type === "add") add++;
      if (r.type === "del") del++;
    }
    return { add, del };
  }, [rows]);
  return (
    <div className="font-mono text-[12.5px] leading-relaxed">
      <div className="sticky top-0 z-10 flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-zinc-700/60 bg-[#161b22] px-3 py-1.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-400">
        <span className="rounded bg-emerald-500/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-emerald-400">
          +{stats.add}
        </span>
        <span className="rounded bg-red-500/20 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-red-400">
          −{stats.del}
        </span>
        <span className="ml-auto">comparaison ligne à ligne</span>
      </div>
      <div className="overflow-x-auto bg-[#0d1117]">
        {rows.map((r, i) => (
          <div
            key={i}
            className={`flex ${
              r.type === "add"
                ? "bg-emerald-500/10"
                : r.type === "del"
                  ? "bg-red-500/10"
                  : ""
            }`}
          >
            <span className="w-10 shrink-0 select-none px-1 text-right text-zinc-600">
              {r.oldLine ?? ""}
            </span>
            <span className="w-10 shrink-0 select-none border-r border-zinc-800 pr-1 text-right text-zinc-600">
              {r.newLine ?? ""}
            </span>
            <span
              className={`w-5 shrink-0 select-none text-center ${
                r.type === "add"
                  ? "text-emerald-400"
                  : r.type === "del"
                    ? "text-red-400"
                    : "text-zinc-700"
              }`}
            >
              {r.type === "add" ? "+" : r.type === "del" ? "−" : " "}
            </span>
            <pre
              className={`flex-1 whitespace-pre-wrap px-2 ${
                r.type === "same" ? "text-zinc-300" : r.type === "add" ? "text-emerald-200" : "text-red-200"
              }`}
            >
              {r.text || " "}
            </pre>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export function CsvTableView({ source }: { source: string }) {
  const rows = useMemo(() => parseCsv(source), [source]);
  if (rows.length === 0) return <p className="p-4 text-sm text-muted-foreground">CSV vide.</p>;
  const [head, ...body] = rows;
  return (
    <div className="overflow-auto">
      <table className="w-full border-collapse text-[12.5px]">
        <thead className="sticky top-0 bg-muted">
          <tr>
            {head.map((c, i) => (
              <th
                key={i}
                className="border-b px-3 py-2 text-left font-semibold whitespace-nowrap"
              >
                {c}
              </th>
            ))}
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody>
          {body.map((r, ri) => (
            <tr key={ri} className={ri % 2 ? "bg-muted/30" : ""}>
              {head.map((_, ci) => (
                <td key={ci} className="border-b px-3 py-1.5 align-top whitespace-nowrap">
                  {r[ci] ?? ""}
                </td>
              ))}
            </tr>
          ))}
        </tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
  );
}

export function JsonView({ source }: { source: string }) {
  const pretty = useMemo(() => {
    try {
      return JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(source), null, 2);
    } catch {
      return source;
    }
  }, [source]);
  return <CodeView source={pretty} language="json" />;
}

/** Source en cours de génération : TEXTE BRUT volontaire (perf 2026-11-19).
 *  L'ancien rendu colorisé re-tokenisait tout le fichier à chaque delta
 *  (~60 Hz, coût quadratique sur les gros artefacts) ; la colorisation
 *  n'arrive qu'à l'état « ready » via CodeView. Mémoïsé sur le contenu :
 *  les re-rendus du panneau (statut, barre) ne re-rendent pas le texte. */
export const LiveSourceView = memo(function LiveSourceView({
  source,
}: {
  source: string;
}) {
  return (
    <pre
      className="whitespace-pre-wrap break-words p-[0.85rem_1rem] text-zinc-300"
      style={{
        margin: 0,
        background: "#0d1117",
        fontSize: "12.5px",
        lineHeight: 1.65,
        minHeight: "100%",
        fontFamily: CODE_FONT_FAMILY,
      }}
    >
      <code>{source}</code>
    </pre>
  );
});

/** Image / aperçu PNG avec zoom (ajuster ↔ 100 %) et déplacement au scroll. */
export function ImageView({
  src,
  alt,
  isGenerating,
  statusLabel,
}: {
  src: string;
  alt: string;
  isGenerating?: boolean;
  statusLabel?: string;
}) {
  const [fit, setFit] = useState(true);
  return (
    <div className="relative flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col">
      <div className="absolute right-2 top-2 z-10 flex gap-1">
        <Button
          variant="secondary"
          size="icon"
          className="h-7 w-7 rounded-lg shadow-sm"
          onClick={() => setFit((f) => !f)}
          aria-label={fit ? "Taille réelle" : "Ajuster à la largeur"}
          title={fit ? "Taille réelle (100 %)" : "Ajuster à la largeur"}
        >
          {fit ? <Maximize2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" /> : <Minimize2 className="h-3.5 w-3.5" />}
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div className="min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-auto bg-[repeating-conic-gradient(var(--muted)_0%_25%,transparent_0%_50%)] bg-[length:16px_16px]">
        {isGenerating ? (
          <div className="flex h-full min-h-64 items-center justify-center gap-2 text-sm text-muted-foreground">
            <Loader2 className="h-4 w-4 animate-spin text-hermes-text" />
            {statusLabel ?? "Rendu de l'aperçu (Chrome)…"}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <img
            src={src}
            alt={alt}
            className={fit ? "w-full" : "w-max"}
            draggable={false}
          />
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

================= FILE: src/components/chat/attachments.tsx =================
// Rendu des pièces jointes multimédia : images, vidéo, audio, PDF, fichiers
"use client";

import { useState } from "react";
import {
  ChevronDown,
  Download,
  FileAudio,
  FileText,
  FileVideo,
  File as FileIcon,
  ImageIcon,
  Loader2,
  Quote,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import {
  Dialog,
  DialogContent,
  DialogTitle,
} from "@/components/ui/dialog";
import { Button } from "@/components/ui/button";
import { serviceUrl } from "@/lib/service";
import type { Attachment } from "@/lib/chat-types";

export function formatBytes(n: number): string {
  if (n < 1024) return `${n} o`;
  if (n < 1024 * 1024) return `${(n / 1024).toFixed(0)} Ko`;
  return `${(n / 1024 / 1024).toFixed(1)} Mo`;
}

function kindIcon(kind: Attachment["kind"], className: string) {
  switch (kind) {
    case "image":
      return <ImageIcon className={className} />;
    case "audio":
      return <FileAudio className={className} />;
    case "video":
      return <FileVideo className={className} />;
    case "pdf":
      return <FileText className={className} />;
    default:
      return <FileIcon className={className} />;
  }
}

/** Vignette d'image cliquable, avec visionneuse plein écran. */
function ImageAttachment({ attachment }: { attachment: Attachment }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [loaded, setLoaded] = useState(false);
  const src = serviceUrl(attachment.url);

  return (
    <>
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={() => setOpen(true)}
        className="group/img relative block max-h-56 overflow-hidden rounded-xl border border-border/60 bg-muted/40 transition-opacity hover:opacity-90"
        aria-label={`Agrandir l'image ${attachment.name}`}
      >
        <img
          src={src}
